=== amd aa8399e5b5e96af2cab80d01aa636c772cdae54e
# Neonbridge launcher spec for vendor/amd: myokex's victory-amd, a VIctory fork for AMD Radeon.
# Build and run are its README.md "Building" (README.md:391-392); its defaults need no switches.
name = "amd"
fork = "VIctory"
gpu = "AMD"   # the card it is tuned for (README "Tuned for"; the picker's filter)
rank = 46   # i95 rank: score 56.5 by PortWatch VI's formula (from the author's report; scripts/rank.py)
about = "victory-amd aa8399e (GPL-3.0): 2 to 3 fps in the world over 8-minute runs, RTX 4070; not yet run here"
upstream = "https://github.com/myokex/victory-amd"
updated = "2026-11-25 18:46"   # the pinned upstream commit's date (scripts/runtime-info.py)
stars = 228   # GitHub stars when last checked
build = ["cargo", "build", "--release", "-p", "victory", "--locked"]   # README.md:391 (+--locked)
watch = ["apps", "crates", "Cargo.toml", "Cargo.lock"]
exe = "target/release/victory.exe"   # README.md:392
run = ["run", "{game_exe}", "--execute"]   # README.md:392
prewarm = ["translate", "{game}", "--warm", "all", "--jobs", "{jobs}"]   # apps/victory/src/translate.rs:160
game_env = "VICTORY_GAME_DIR"
cache_env = "VICTORY_CACHE_DIR"
agility = false

options = [
  "VICTORY_RELAY=2, or VICTORY_LATE_WAIT=3 VICTORY_WIDE_BACK=1 VICTORY_CARD_BUFFERS_MIN_MB=1 VICTORY_CARD_BUFFERS_MB=1024: 240-350 ms a frame (README.md:46)",
  "VICTORY_WAVE_WIDE=1: wave64 barrier form, off since it hung the driver (README.md:47)",
]

preset_series_s = ["--console=series-s"]   # Series S profile (less GPU memory)
preset_dred = ["NEONBRIDGE_DIAG=1", "VICTORY_D3D12_DRED=1"]   # D3D12 crash breadcrumbs in the log (diagnostics build: host.rs reads it only there)
preset_warp = ["NEONBRIDGE_DIAG=1", "VICTORY_D3D12_WARP=1"]   # software GPU (slow; diagnostics build only, host.rs cfg!(feature = "diagnostics"))

[env]

# Neonbridge launcher spec for vendor/anyxboxx: LooperSalty's AnyXboxX. Its game runtime is the VIctory
# copy in third_party/victory (now built on Vice-Tay, README.md 5a08a33). The author's default is `anyxboxx run --game-dir <game>` (README.md:43), a Rust
# wrapper (crates/anyxboxx/src/run) that builds victory, sets its TUNED env and spawns victory.exe. Replicated
# here, not wrapped: the wrapper builds victory itself and can't take i95's warm placeholders.

# NVIDIA Streamline SDK v2.14.16 (the version its author tested) from NVIDIA-RTX/Streamline releases, its bin\x64 into %LOCALAPPDATA%\6ixpack\streamline for the DLSS presets
setup = [
  "streamline: powershell -NoProfile -Command \"$d=Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA '6ixpack\\streamline'; if (-not (Test-Path (Join-Path $d 'sl.interposer.dll'))) { $t=Join-Path $env:TEMP 'streamline-sdk-v2.14.16.zip'; Invoke-WebRequest -UseBasicParsing https://github.com/NVIDIA-RTX/Streamline/releases/download/v2.14.16/streamline-sdk-v2.14.16.zip -OutFile $t; $x=Join-Path $env:TEMP 'streamline-sdk-v2.14.16'; Expand-Archive -Force $t $x; New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $d | Out-Null; $b=(Get-ChildItem $x -Recurse -Directory -Filter x64 | Where-Object { $_.Parent.Name -eq 'bin' } | Select-Object -First 1).FullName; Copy-Item (Join-Path $b '*') $d -Recurse -Force; Remove-Item -Recurse -Force $t,$x }\" [asks first: download]",
]
